<h2 class="relative group flex items-baseline">
	<a id="multi-turn-conversations" class="block pr-1.5 text-lg md:absolute md:p-1.5 md:opacity-0 md:group-hover:opacity-100 md:right-full" href="#multi-turn-conversations" rel="nofollow">
		<span class="header-link"><svg class="text-gray-500 hover:text-black dark:hover:text-gray-200 w-4" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg></span>
	</a>
	<span>
		Multi-Turn Conversations
	</span>
</h2>
<h3 class="relative group flex items-baseline">
	<a id="history-thinking-truncation" class="block pr-1.5 text-lg md:absolute md:p-1.5 md:opacity-0 md:group-hover:opacity-100 md:right-full" href="#history-thinking-truncation" rel="nofollow">
		<span class="header-link"><svg class="text-gray-500 hover:text-black dark:hover:text-gray-200 w-4" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg></span>
	</a>
	<span>
		History Thinking Truncation
	</span>
</h3>
<pre><code class="language-python">messages = [
    {<span class="hljs-string">"role"</span>: <span class="hljs-string">"user"</span>, <span class="hljs-string">"content"</span>: <span class="hljs-string">"What is 15 * 37?"</span>},
    {<span class="hljs-string">"role"</span>: <span class="hljs-string">"assistant"</span>, <span class="hljs-string">"content"</span>: <span class="hljs-string">"&lt;think&gt;\nLet me calculate 15 * 37.\n15 * 37 = 15 * 30 + 15 * 7 = 450 + 105 = 555\n&lt;/think&gt;\n15 * 37 = 555"</span>},
    {<span class="hljs-string">"role"</span>: <span class="hljs-string">"user"</span>, <span class="hljs-string">"content"</span>: <span class="hljs-string">"Now divide that by 5"</span>},
]

<span class="hljs-comment"># Default: previous thinking is stripped to save context</span>
text = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(messages, tokenize=<span class="hljs-literal">False</span>, add_generation_prompt=<span class="hljs-literal">True</span>,
                                     enable_thinking=<span class="hljs-literal">True</span>, truncate_history_thinking=<span class="hljs-literal">True</span>)

<span class="hljs-comment"># To preserve full history:</span>
text_full = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(messages, tokenize=<span class="hljs-literal">False</span>, add_generation_prompt=<span class="hljs-literal">True</span>,
                                          enable_thinking=<span class="hljs-literal">True</span>, truncate_history_thinking=<span class="hljs-literal">False</span>)
</code></pre>
<h3 class="relative group flex items-baseline">
	<a id="parsing-thinking-vs-final-answer" class="block pr-1.5 text-lg md:absolute md:p-1.5 md:opacity-0 md:group-hover:opacity-100 md:right-full" href="#parsing-thinking-vs-final-answer" rel="nofollow">
		<span class="header-link"><svg class="text-gray-500 hover:text-black dark:hover:text-gray-200 w-4" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg></span>
	</a>
	<span>
		Parsing Thinking vs. Final Answer
	</span>
</h3>
<pre><code class="language-python"><span class="hljs-keyword">import</span> re

<span class="hljs-keyword">def</span> <span class="hljs-title function_">parse_model_output</span>(<span class="hljs-params">text</span>):
    <span class="hljs-string">"""Separate thinking content from final answer."""</span>
    think_match = re.search(<span class="hljs-string">r'&lt;think&gt;(.*?)&lt;/think&gt;'</span>, text, re.DOTALL)
    <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> think_match:
        thinking = think_match.group(<span class="hljs-number">1</span>).strip()
        answer_start = text.find(<span class="hljs-string">'&lt;/think&gt;'</span>) + <span class="hljs-built_in">len</span>(<span class="hljs-string">'&lt;/think&gt;'</span>)
        answer_end = text.find(<span class="hljs-string">'&lt;|im_end|&gt;'</span>, answer_start)
        answer = text[answer_start:answer_end].strip() <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> answer_end != -<span class="hljs-number">1</span> <span class="hljs-keyword">else</span> text[answer_start:].strip()
    <span class="hljs-keyword">else</span>:
        thinking, answer = <span class="hljs-string">""</span>, text.strip()
    <span class="hljs-keyword">return</span> thinking, answer

thinking, answer = parse_model_output(output_text)
</code></pre>
<hr>
<h2 class="relative group flex items-baseline">
	<a id="using-with-agentic-coding-harnesses" class="block pr-1.5 text-lg md:absolute md:p-1.5 md:opacity-0 md:group-hover:opacity-100 md:right-full" href="#using-with-agentic-coding-harnesses" rel="nofollow">
		<span class="header-link"><svg class="text-gray-500 hover:text-black dark:hover:text-gray-200 w-4" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg></span>
	</a>
	<span>
		Using with Agentic Coding Harnesses
	</span>
</h2>
<p>Granite models can serve as the backbone for agentic coding tools. Because they support reasoning and tool calling through the OpenAI-compatible API, they integrate with popular agentic harnesses without extra adapters. Start the vLLM server, then follow the harness-specific instructions below.</p>
<h3 class="relative group flex items-baseline">
	<a id="opencode" class="block pr-1.5 text-lg md:absolute md:p-1.5 md:opacity-0 md:group-hover:opacity-100 md:right-full" href="#opencode" rel="nofollow">
		<span class="header-link"><svg class="text-gray-500 hover:text-black dark:hover:text-gray-200 w-4" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg></span>
	</a>
	<span>
		OpenCode
	</span>
</h3>
<p><a href="https://opencode.ai" rel="nofollow">OpenCode</a> is an AI coding agent that runs in your terminal.</p>
<p><strong>Install:</strong></p>
<pre><code class="language-bash">curl -fsSL https://opencode.ai/install | bash
</code></pre>
<p><strong>Configure</strong> <code>~/.config/opencode/opencode.json</code>:</p>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">"$schema"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">"https://opencode.ai/config.json"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">"model"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">"local/granite-4.2-30b"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">"provider"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
    <span class="hljs-attr">"local"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
      <span class="hljs-attr">"npm"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">"@ai-sdk/openai-compatible"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
      <span class="hljs-attr">"name"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">"vLLM (local)"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
      <span class="hljs-attr">"options"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
        <span class="hljs-attr">"baseURL"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">"http://localhost:8000/v1"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
        <span class="hljs-attr">"apiKey"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">"EMPTY"</span>
      <span class="hljs-punctuation">}</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
      <span class="hljs-attr">"models"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
        <span class="hljs-attr">"granite-4.2-30b"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
          <span class="hljs-attr">"name"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">"Granite 4.2 30B"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
          <span class="hljs-attr">"limit"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
            <span class="hljs-attr">"context"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-number">131072</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
            <span class="hljs-attr">"output"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-number">8192</span>
          <span class="hljs-punctuation">}</span>
        <span class="hljs-punctuation">}</span>
      <span class="hljs-punctuation">}</span>
    <span class="hljs-punctuation">}</span>
  <span class="hljs-punctuation">}</span>
<span class="hljs-punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre>
<p><strong>Run:</strong></p>
<pre><code class="language-bash">opencode
opencode run <span class="hljs-string">"your task description"</span>
</code></pre>
<p>For full documentation, see <a href="https://opencode.ai/docs" rel="nofollow">opencode.ai/docs</a>.</p>
<h3 class="relative group flex items-baseline">
	<a id="pi" class="block pr-1.5 text-lg md:absolute md:p-1.5 md:opacity-0 md:group-hover:opacity-100 md:right-full" href="#pi" rel="nofollow">
		<span class="header-link"><svg class="text-gray-500 hover:text-black dark:hover:text-gray-200 w-4" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg></span>
	</a>
	<span>
		Pi
	</span>
</h3>
<p><a href="https://pi.dev" rel="nofollow">Pi</a> is a minimal agent harness for AI-powered coding that runs in your terminal. It supports custom providers via a <code>models.json</code> configuration file.</p>
<p><strong>Install:</strong></p>
<pre><code class="language-bash">curl -fsSL https://pi.dev/install.sh | sh
</code></pre>
<p><strong>Configure</strong> <code>~/.pi/agent/models.json</code>:</p>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">"providers"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
    <span class="hljs-attr">"vllm"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
      <span class="hljs-attr">"baseUrl"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">"http://localhost:8000/v1"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
      <span class="hljs-attr">"api"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">"openai-completions"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
      <span class="hljs-attr">"apiKey"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">"EMPTY"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
      <span class="hljs-attr">"compat"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
        <span class="hljs-attr">"supportsDeveloperRole"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-literal"><span class="hljs-keyword">false</span></span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
        <span class="hljs-attr">"supportsReasoningEffort"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-literal"><span class="hljs-keyword">false</span></span>
      <span class="hljs-punctuation">}</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
      <span class="hljs-attr">"models"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">[</span>
        <span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
          <span class="hljs-attr">"id"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">"granite-4.2-30b"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
          <span class="hljs-attr">"name"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">"Granite 4.2 30B"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
          <span class="hljs-attr">"reasoning"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-literal"><span class="hljs-keyword">true</span></span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
          <span class="hljs-attr">"input"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">[</span><span class="hljs-string">"text"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">]</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
          <span class="hljs-attr">"contextWindow"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-number">131072</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
          <span class="hljs-attr">"maxTokens"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-number">8192</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
          <span class="hljs-attr">"samplingParams"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
            <span class="hljs-attr">"temperature"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-number">1.0</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
            <span class="hljs-attr">"top_p"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-number">0.95</span>
          <span class="hljs-punctuation">}</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
          <span class="hljs-attr">"cost"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span> <span class="hljs-attr">"input"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-number">0</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> <span class="hljs-attr">"output"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-number">0</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> <span class="hljs-attr">"cacheRead"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-number">0</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> <span class="hljs-attr">"cacheWrite"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-number">0</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">}</span>
        <span class="hljs-punctuation">}</span>
      <span class="hljs-punctuation">]</span>
    <span class="hljs-punctuation">}</span>
  <span class="hljs-punctuation">}</span>
<span class="hljs-punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre>
<p><strong>Run:</strong></p>
<pre><code class="language-bash">pi
</code></pre>
<p>Then select the <code>granite-4.2-30b</code> model with <code>/model</code> or <code>Ctrl+L</code> in the interactive session.</p>
<p>For full documentation, see <a href="https://pi.dev/docs/latest" rel="nofollow">pi.dev/docs</a>.</p>
<h3 class="relative group flex items-baseline">
	<a id="openhands" class="block pr-1.5 text-lg md:absolute md:p-1.5 md:opacity-0 md:group-hover:opacity-100 md:right-full" href="#openhands" rel="nofollow">
		<span class="header-link"><svg class="text-gray-500 hover:text-black dark:hover:text-gray-200 w-4" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" aria-hidden="true" role="img" width="1em" height="1em" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" viewBox="0 0 256 256"><path d="M167.594 88.393a8.001 8.001 0 0 1 0 11.314l-67.882 67.882a8 8 0 1 1-11.314-11.315l67.882-67.881a8.003 8.003 0 0 1 11.314 0zm-28.287 84.86l-28.284 28.284a40 40 0 0 1-56.567-56.567l28.284-28.284a8 8 0 0 0-11.315-11.315l-28.284 28.284a56 56 0 0 0 79.196 79.197l28.285-28.285a8 8 0 1 0-11.315-11.314zM212.852 43.14a56.002 56.002 0 0 0-79.196 0l-28.284 28.284a8 8 0 1 0 11.314 11.314l28.284-28.284a40 40 0 0 1 56.568 56.567l-28.285 28.285a8 8 0 0 0 11.315 11.314l28.284-28.284a56.065 56.065 0 0 0 0-79.196z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg></span>
	</a>
	<span>
		OpenHands
	</span>
</h3>
<p><a href="https://www.openhands.dev" rel="nofollow">OpenHands</a> is an AI software engineer that can plan, write code, and execute commands.</p>
<ol>
<li><p><strong>Install and launch OpenHands</strong> following the <a href="https://docs.openhands.dev/openhands/usage/agent-canvas/setup" rel="nofollow">official installation guide</a>.</p>
</li>
<li><p><strong>Configure the LLM</strong> in the OpenHands settings with:</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Model:</strong> <code>granite-4.2-30b</code></li>
<li><strong>Base URL:</strong> <code>http://localhost:8000/v1</code></li>
<li><strong>API Key:</strong> your vLLM <code>--api-key</code> value</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
<blockquote>
<p><strong>Note:</strong> The <code>openai/</code> prefix is required when connecting to OpenAI-compatible endpoints like vLLM. Refer to the <a href="https://docs.openhands.dev/openhands/usage/llms/local-llms" rel="nofollow">OpenHands local LLM documentation</a> for detailed setup instructions, troubleshooting, and alternative installation methods.</p>
</blockquote>
